  "tool_args": {
    "text": "\n## Reviewer 2 审稿报告：新颖性 + 完整性审查\n\n**论文**: \"Beyond Self-Consistency: Self-Extracted Constraints Do Not Substantially Improve LLM Logical Reasoning\"\n**Venue**: EMNLP 2026 via ACL Rolling Review\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\n本文引入 SICA（Symbolic Invariant Consensus Aggregation），从 LLM 的 chain-of-thought traces 中提取 FOL 约束并通过加权 MAX-SAT 聚合。尽管 Z3 验证通过率达 99%，SICA 在 94.4% 的问题上与多数投票结果一致（6 个模型，7B-32B，3 个推理领域）。论文将此现象形式化为 \"Symbolic Confirmation Bias\"（SCB），基于 Data Processing Inequality（DPI）证明 BR ≥ 1（Proposition 1），表明自提取约束无法超越 trace 本身的信息量。测试 27 个 training-free 变体均产生中性至负面结果。提出三个零成本诊断工具（C₀, BR, K_eff）并展示跨架构验证作为突破瓶颈的方向。\n\n---\n\n## 主要优势\n\n**S1. 实验规模与方法论严谨性出色。** 9 个模型（7B-32B）、3 个推理领域（FOLIO, ProofWriter, StrategyQA/LogiQA/AIME/ARC-Challenge）、27 个 training-free 变体的全面覆盖。统计方法完备：TOST 等价性检验、McNemar 检验、bootstrap CI、FDR 校正、统计功效分析。这是该领域最系统的 negative result 研究之一。\n\n**S2. 诊断框架具有实际价值。** C₀（多候选退化率）、BR（偏差比）、K_eff（有效集成大小）三个指标可在不运行完整实验的情况下预测 self-extraction 方法的成败，对从业者具有直接指导意义（§5.3, Figure 4）。\n\n**S3. 因果分析链条清晰。** 从现象（SICA ≈ SC）到机制（trace-count 分布决定约束分布）到理论（DPI Markov chain）到逃逸条件（跨架构独立性），叙事层层递进。Figure 3 的因果 DAG 和 Figure 1 的 pipeline 图有效辅助理解。\n\n**S4. 跨架构逃逸发现有启示性。** §6 的 cross-generator NLI 实验（Table 3）展示独立验证器可将 SC 提升至 72%+，为后续研究指明了清晰方向——这从 negative result 中提炼出了 constructive 的贡献。\n\n**S5. 诚实的统计报告。** 论文坦承 10/16 条件统计功效不足（power < 21%），并在 Limitations 中明确列出模型范围、语言、数据集等局限。这种透明度在 negative result 论文中值得肯定。\n\n---\n\n## Must-Fix 问题（影响接收决定）\n\n**N1** [critical] 标题的普遍性声明与统计证据不匹配\n\n- 具体位置：Title, Abstract (\"We show the answer is no\"), §4 Table 1\n- 问题：标题 \"Do Not Substantially Improve\" 暗示一个普遍性结论，但论文自身的 TOST 分析（Appendix D, Table 6）显示：仅 4/16 主条件通过等价性检验（±2pp），2 个条件显示显著负面效果，**10 个条件因统计功效不足（power < 21%）无法判定**。Abstract 声称 \"four conditions confirm equivalence within ±2pp, though ten remain underpowered\" 但紧接着用 \"We show the answer is no\" 做总结，逻辑上不一致——underpowered ≠ equivalent。\n- 为什么影响接收：Reviewer 和读者会将标题作为 take-home message。在 10/16 条件 underpowered 的情况下做普遍性声明，有 overclaim 风险。EMNLP/ARR 对 claim 与 evidence 的匹配要求严格。\n- 建议修改方向：标题改为限定性表述，如 \"Self-Extracted Constraints Show No Significant Improvement...\"；或在 Abstract 中明确区分 \"confirmed equivalent\" vs. \"underpowered/inconclusive\"，不将两者混为一谈。\n\n**N2** [critical] Proposition 1 的 Markov chain 假设缺乏显式论证\n\n- 具体位置：§5.1 Proposition 1, Figure 3 (p6-7)\n- 问题：Proposition 1 的核心依赖 Markov chain M → T → A → C → S，其中 C（约束）条件独立于 M（模型）given T（trace）。但 SICA pipeline 中，约束提取使用**同一模型**的**另一次 LLM 调用**重新处理 trace（Appendix A 的 extraction prompt）。如果同一模型生成 trace 并提取约束，模型的 shared latent representations（权重、attention patterns）可能导致 C 和 M 之间存在 T 无法屏蔽的依赖关系，违反条件独立性。论文 §5.1 讨论了 \"trace-determined\" 但未直接论证 Markov 性质为何成立。\n- 为什么影响接收：Proposition 1 是全文理论框架的基石。如果 Markov 假设不成立，BR ≥ 1 的保证不再有效，整个 \"structural\" argument 需要重新审视。\n- 建议修改方向：(1) 明确论证在什么条件下 Markov 假设成立（例如，extraction prompt 只访问 trace 文本而非模型内部状态）；(2) 讨论假设可能失效的场景（例如，模型在 extraction 时使用与 trace generation 相同的 in-context learning patterns）；(3) 如果假设是近似的，量化偏离程度。\n\n**N3** [critical] SCB 与已有 confirmation bias 工作的新颖性区分不充分\n\n- 具体位置：§2 Related Work (p2-3), §5.1 (p5-6)\n- 问题：Wan et al. (2025, ACL Findings) 的 \"Unveiling Confirmation Bias in Chain-of-Thought Reasoning\" 已经证明 model beliefs 通过 CoT 过程产生 confirmation bias，扭曲推理生成和答案预测。本文的 SCB 本质上是同一现象在 FOL/constraint extraction 载体上的实例化。论文 §2 引用了 Wan et al. 但仅一句带过（\"symbolic confirmation bias is distinct from CoT unfaithfulness\"），未直接对比 SCB 与 Wan et al. 的 CoT confirmation bias 在概念上的重叠与差异。同样，Jhaveri & Kersting (2026) 在 rule-discovery 任务中发现类似 confirmation bias，进一步削弱了 SCB 作为独立发现的新颖性。\n- 为什么影响接收：如果 SCB 是 CoT confirmation bias 的直接推论而非独立发现，论文的核心贡献从 \"发现新现象 + 形式化\" 降级为 \"在新载体上验证已知现象 + 形式化\"。这仍有价值但需要诚实定位。\n- 建议修改方向：在 §2 或 §5.1 中增加显式对比段落：(1) Wan et al. 的 CoT confirmation bias 是关于推理过程的 behavioral 观察；(2) SCB 的增量贡献在于 DPI 形式化和 source-attribution scoring 机制，以及在 formal verification pipeline 中的具体表现；(3) 明确承认 SCB 是 confirmation bias 在 symbolic verification 中的具体实例化，而非全新概念。\n\n---\n\n## Should-Fix 问题（影响评分但不一票否决）\n\n**W1** [warning] DPI 推论（Proposition 1 + Corollary 1）的理论贡献度偏弱\n\n- 具体位置：§5.1 Proposition 1, Corollary 1 (p6)\n- 问题：给定 Markov chain M → T → A → C → S，BR ≥ 1 和 I(C;Y) ≤ I(T;Y) 是 DPI 的直接应用，推导过程本身不含非显然步骤。Corollary 1 尤其接近 trivial——\"从 trace 提取的约束所含信息不超过 trace 本身\"几乎是同义反复。论文将其列为第一个贡献（\"We systematically diagnose symbolic confirmation bias as the mechanism\"），但理论新颖性主要在于*选择了正确的 Markov chain 建模*，而非推导本身。\n- 建议：在论文中明确区分贡献的层次——modeling contribution（识别正确的 DAG/Markov chain）vs. derivation contribution（DPI 应用），并适当降低 Corollary 1 的声称强度。\n\n**W2** [warning] 诊断框架的 \"100% accuracy\" 声称部分为 in-sample\n\n- 具体位置：§5.3 (p7), Abstract (\"Three zero-cost diagnostics correctly classify all 60 tested conditions\"), Figure 4\n- 问题：三个诊断工具的决策边界（C₀ ≤ .52, K_eff ≤ 1.30, BR combined with SC regime）是从实验数据中拟合的，而 \"100% accuracy\" 的评估也在同一数据集上。虽然 §5.3 提到在两个 held-out 模型系列（LLaMA-3.1-8B, Qwen3-14B）上做了前瞻性验证，但 held-out 的是模型而非条件设计，且仅 2 个模型系列。\n- 建议：(1) 明确标注哪些结果是 in-sample fitting、哪些是 out-of-sample validation；(2) 将 \"correctly classify all 60 conditions\" 改为 \"correctly classify all 60 conditions in our experimental setting, including prospective validation on 2 held-out model families\"；(3) 讨论边界在分布外场景（如 >32B 模型、非英语、非 FOL 领域）的预期鲁棒性。\n\n**W3** [warning] Scope 震荡：SICA-specific claims vs. 一般性 claims\n\n- 具体位置：§1 contributions (p2), §5.2 (p7), §7 (p8)\n- 问题：论文在两个 scope 之间摆动：(a) \"SICA fails\"（specific）和 (b) \"any self-extraction-based aggregation method within the constraint-extraction-aggregation paradigm fails\"（general）。Proposition 1 的理论声称是 (b)，但 27 个变体的实验验证主要是 SICA 及其衍生方法（均在同一 extraction-aggregation paradigm 内）。§5.2 声称 \"the pattern reveals a fundamental independence-accuracy trade-off\" 暗示普遍性，但 §7 的 Discussion 又缩回 \"within the constraint-extraction-aggregation paradigm\"。\n- 建议：在 §1 的 contributions 中明确界定 scope：\"我们的理论结果适用于任何 trace-to-constraint extraction pipeline（Proposition 1）；我们的实验验证覆盖了该 paradigm 内的 27 个代表性变体\"。\n\n**W4** [warning] K_eff 对非 ensemble-theory 背景读者的可读性不足\n\n- 具体位置：§3.1 Eq. (1) (p3)\n- 问题：K_eff = K/[1+(K-1)·κ] 通过 Fleiss' κ 和 Ladha (1992) 引入，但对不熟悉 ensemble diversity 理论的读者（NLP 社区中占多数），缺少直觉解释。K_eff 是论文的核心诊断指标之一，理解障碍会影响论文的可读性和影响力。\n- 建议：在 Eq. (1) 后加一个具体示例，如 \"With K=12 traces and inter-trace agreement κ=0.7, the effective ensemble size reduces to K_eff ≈ 1.3, meaning 12 highly correlated traces carry roughly the same information as 1.3 independent ones.\"\n\n**W5** [warning] \"Symbolic Mirage\" 术语过度品牌化\n\n- 具体位置：§5 标题 (p5)\n- 问题：\"The Symbolic Mirage\" 作为 section 标题带有不必要的修辞色彩。论文已有 \"Symbolic Confirmation Bias\" 这一技术性术语来描述同一现象。\"Mirage\" 暗示一种刻意的欺骗性，与实际的技术机制（trace-count 分布决定约束分布）不匹配。\n- 建议：将 §5 标题改为更技术性的表述，如 \"Analysis of Symbolic Confirmation Bias\" 或 \"Why Self-Extraction Degenerates\"。\n\n---\n\n## 小问题（不影响接收）\n\n- **符号过载**：正文 8 页内引入了 BR, K_eff, C₀, κ, σ, α, Δ, score(a), CCR, ProcessSim 等大量符号/指标。建议将 CCR 和 ProcessSim 的定义移至 Appendix，正文仅引用结论。\n- **Abstract 信息密度过高**：一个段落包含 ~6 个核心发现。建议以中心 negative finding 和机制为主线，将 scope 和 escape 发现精简为 1-2 句。\n- **§2 Related Work 段间衔接**：三个子节（Self-consistency/ensemble theory, Neuro-symbolic reasoning, Test-time compute/self-verification）之间缺少显式逻辑连接句。读者需自行推断各节与本文研究问题的关系。\n- **Table 1 排版密度**：Table 1（p5）压缩了大量信息（11 个模型-领域条件 × 5 列），字体较小。考虑拆分为两个子表或增加水平间距。\n- **Appendix 表格编号跳跃**：例如从 Table 8 跳到 Table 9，再到 Table 12，编号不连续可能造成交叉引用困惑。\n\n---\n\n## Questions to Authors\n\n**Q1.** Proposition 1 依赖 M→T→A→C→S 的 Markov chain。当 extraction 使用同一模型的另一次调用时，模型权重构成 C 和 M 之间的隐式依赖。你们是否有实验证据表明这种依赖在实践中可忽略（例如，用不同模型做 extraction 是否改变 BR 的分布）？\n\n**Q2.** 10/16 条件的统计功效不足（power < 21%）。如果将样本量增加到使 power 达到 80% 的水平（对 ±2pp 效应），你们预期结论是否会改变？是否有 pilot 数据暗示某些 underpowered 条件实际上可能显示正面效果？\n\n**Q3.** 诊断框架在 >32B 模型（如 LLaMA-3-70B, Qwen-72B）上的预期表现如何？K_eff 和 BR 是否会因模型能力提升而表现出不同的分布？论文 Limitations 提到模型范围 7B-32B，但不清楚这是资源限制还是有理论原因预期更大模型表现不同。\n\n**Q4.** Cross-architecture escape（§6）中，DeBERTa-large-mnli 作为验证器在 FOLIO 上将 SC 提升至 ~49.5%（Table 3）。但该验证器本身是在 NLI 数据上 fine-tune 的，这是否引入了 Proposition 1 不覆盖的 external supervision signal？换言之，这个 \"escape\" 是否本质上是因为违反了 Proposition 1 的前提（无外部反馈），而非突破了 information ceiling？\n\n---\n\n## Missing References\n\n1. **Wan et al. (2025)** — 论文已引用但讨论不足。SCB 与 CoT confirmation bias 的关系需要更深入的对比分析（见 N3）。\n2. **Reflexion (Shinn et al., 2023)** — 作为 verbal self-reflection 的代表性工作，在 self-verification landscape 中占重要位置，但论文未提及。\n3. **LATS (Zhou et al., 2023)** — Language Agent Tree Search 通过树搜索实现 self-verification，是 training-free verification 的另一范式，可能不在 constraint-extraction paradigm 内，但值得在 Related Work 中定位。\n4. **STaR (Zelikman et al., 2022)** — Self-Taught Reasoner 是 training-based self-improvement 的奠基性工作。论文讨论了 training-based verification 但未引用此具体工作。\n5. 建议检查 Kuai et al. (2026) 的引用深度——论文引用了但仅在 Related Work 一笔带过，考虑到 cross-model independence 是本文 §6 的核心 escape mechanism，应更充分讨论其方法与发现。\n\n---\n\n## 格式合规\n\n- **页数**：正文 8 页（含 Limitations），合规 ✓\n- **Limitations**：存在，位于 §7 Discussion and Conclusion 之后、References 之前，合规 ✓\n- **匿名性**：首页标注 \"Anonymous ACL submission\"，未发现明显违反 double-blind 的信息 ✓\n- **引用格式**：使用 natbib 风格，parenthetical 和 textual citations 混合使用，合规 ✓\n- **Appendix**：18 页 appendix 提供详尽的实验细节、prompts、额外分析，增强可重复性 ✓\n\n---\n\n## 官方维度打分（ACL ARR Review Form）\n\n| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |\n|------|------|----------|\n| Soundness | 3.5/5 | 实验方法论严谨（TOST, bootstrap CI, power analysis），但 Proposition 1 的 Markov chain 假设未充分论证（N2），标题与统计证据不完全匹配（N1） |\n| Excitement | 3.0/5 | Negative result 有价值，诊断框架和跨架构发现有实际意义，但核心发现（自提取约束受 confirmation bias 影响）与 Wan et al. (2025) 等已有工作重叠较大（N3），降低了新颖性感知 |\n| Reproducibility | 4/5 | 提供完整 prompt（Appendix A）、超参数设置（§3.2）、统计方法细节（Appendix D），但未提及代码/数据公开计划 |\n| Confidence | 4/5 | Self-verification 和 neuro-symbolic reasoning 是我的研究领域，对统计方法和实验设计有充分把握 |\n\n## 总体评分\n\n**Overall**: 3.5 — Borderline Conference\n\n**一句话总结**: 实验设计出色的 negative result 研究，但理论贡献度（DPI 直接应用）和核心发现新颖性（与 CoT confirmation bias 重叠）不足以支撑 conference-level 论文；跨架构逃逸发现和诊断框架是亮点，建议加强新颖性区分后适合 Findings。\n\n**作为 negative result 论文的价值评估**: 本文在失败机制分析深度（Proposition 1 的 DPI 框架 + BR/K_eff 的实证验证）和实验覆盖广度（27 变体）方面超越了大多数 negative result 工作。诊断框架对社区有实际指导意义——从业者可以用 C₀ 和 K_eff 在 pilot run 中快速判断 self-extraction 方法是否值得全面部署。independence principle 为后续工作提供了明确方向。但如果核心洞察可以总结为 \"self-extracted constraints inherit the model's distributional prior\"——这在 Huang et al. (2024) 和 Wan et al. (2025) 之后已非 surprising——则论文的主要贡献在于 rigor of confirmation 而非 novelty of insight，更适合 Findings track。\n",
    "reviewer_id": "2",
    "project": "symb_invariant_consensus"
  }
}
```

(2025) 的 CoT confirmation bias 新颖性区分不充分。实验规模和方法论严谨性是明确优势，诊断框架和跨架构逃逸发现有实际价值，建议加强新颖性定位后适合 Findings。

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
以下是 Reviewer 1 的完整审稿意见，请逐条回应：对每个 Must-Fix/Should-Fix/Minor 点标注 [AGREE] 同意并说明理由，或 [REBUT] 反驳并提供证据（cite 具体 Section/Table/Figure）。

---

## Reviewer 1 完整审稿原文

### Must-Fix 问题

**N1** [critical] Proposition 1 证明存在关键逻辑跳跃
- 证明声称 "By definition, score(a) = Σ 1[aᵢ = a] = nₐ"，但 SICA 的实际评分函数（Eq. 3）是 score(a) = Σ_{c∈C*} wc·σ(c,a) − α·Σ_{c∈C\} wc·σ(c,a)，涉及约束权重 wc 和源归属指示函数 σ(c,a)。从 Eq. 3 到 score(a) = nₐ 需要证明：(1) 退化条件下 |C\| ≈ 0 使惩罚项消失；(2) 不同答案组的约束不重叠；(3) 每条 trace 的有效约束贡献使 Σ wc 精确等于 nₐ 而非仅成比例。论文报告 42.9% 去重率（§3.1），这意味着约束权重分布不均匀。证明跳过了这些中间步骤。

**N2** [critical] 统计功效不足覆盖面过大，削弱否定结论的普遍性
- 16 个条件中 10 个 underpowered（MDE ≥ 1.86pp）。GSM8K (n=30) 和 AIME (n=22) 样本量极小，在 Table 1 中与 n=204 条件并列。论文无法区分"SICA 无效"和"样本不够大"。

**N3** [critical] 诊断框架 100% 准确率可能是 trivial classifier + in-sample overfitting
- 60 个条件全部为 null 或 negative，trivial classifier（"总是预测无效果"）也能达到 100%。Holdout 验证仅 2 个条件，且同为 negative cases。

### Should-Fix 问题

**W1** [warning] Corollary 1 (DPI) 贡献定位不清
- I(C;Y) ≤ I(T;Y) 几乎是信息论教科书练习题。

**W2** [warning] Bias Ratio (BR) 缺少 null model 分解
- 60% trace 产生答案 a 时，纯随机约束-答案关联也会产生 BR = 1.5。需 permutation null model 量化 excess bias。

**W3** [warning] Cross-architecture escape (§6) 结论过度推断
- 仅 6 个条件，Spearman ρ=1.0 (n=6) 鲁棒性存疑。"capability-gap pattern" 数据不足。

**W4** [warning] α=0.5 无理论依据，sensitivity 分析实质上确认了退化
- ≥99% agreement 是 ceiling effect 而非鲁棒性。

**W5** [warning] Multi-seed replication 覆盖率不足
- 仅 2/6 模型有 multi-seed。Mistral-7B 的 Δ ∈ [-4.41, +3.43] 跨越正负方向。

### Questions

**Q1**: score(a) = nₐ 如何从 Eq. 3 精确推导？
**Q2**: 诊断框架的附加值（over trivial baseline）具体体现在哪里？
**Q3**: TOST ±2pp margin 的先验依据？
**Q4**: §6 cross-architecture 实验中 α 是否也固定为 0.5？
**Q5**: Table 2 Gemini BR=12.19 的异常值原因？

### Missing References
- Cohen's h 或其他标准化效应量
- Burnell et al. (2023) "Rethink reporting of evaluation results in AI"

---

请逐条回应。

{
  "text": "\n## Reviewer 2 对 Reviewer 1 审稿意见的逐条回应\n\n---\n\n### Must-Fix 问题\n\n**[AGREE] R1-N1: Proposition 1 证明存在关键逻辑跳跃**\n\n同意这是一个实质性的证明缺陷。论文 p6 的证明直接写 \"score(a) = Σ wᵢ = nₐ\"，但这一等式在 SICA 的实际评分机制下不成立。具体分析：\n\n1. **权重与计数的混淆**：§3.1 (p3) 定义 wₖ = \"the number of source traces\"（该去重约束出现在多少条 trace 中），所以 Σ wc ≠ nₐ，而是 Σ wc = 所有来自答案 a 的 (trace, constraint) 对的总数。如果每条 trace 平均生成 m 条约束，则 Σ wc = m · nₐ，是 *比例* 关系而非 *等式*。\n2. **42.9% 去重率的影响**：不同答案组的约束去重率可能不同。如果多数答案组的约束高度重复（高去重率，少数唯一约束但高权重）而少数答案组约束更多样，则 score(a)/score(a') 不再精确等于 nₐ/nₐ'。\n3. **但结论可能仍然成立**：BR ≥ 1 只需要 score(majority answer) ≥ score(any other answer)，这在 \"约束按比例反映 trace 分布\" 的合理假设下仍然成立。问题是 **证明的严谨性**，而非结论的正确性。\n\n**补充**：我在自己的审稿中也指出了 Markov chain 假设 M→T→A→C→S 缺乏显式论证（我的 N2）。R1 的 N1 从评分函数层面、我的 N2 从条件独立性层面分别暴露了 Proposition 1 的不同弱点——两者叠加使该定理的可靠性进一步存疑。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-N2: 统计功效不足覆盖面过大**\n\n完全同意，且与我的 N1（标题 overclaim）一致。补充几点：\n\n1. **GSM8K (n=30) 和 AIME (n=22) 不应出现在主结果中**：这些样本量下，即使效应为 5pp 也检测不到（Table 6, Appendix D）。将它们与 n=204 的 FOLIO 条件并列展示，给读者造成 \"16 个条件都说 no\" 的错误印象。建议将极小样本条件移至 Appendix 作为 exploratory evidence。\n2. **Table 1 的呈现方式加剧问题**：Table 1 用 \"†gold ICL\" 和 \"◊seed-confirmatory\" 等符号区分条件类型，但未在表内标注每个条件的统计功效。读者需要翻到 Appendix D 才能发现 10/16 条件 underpowered。\n3. **与我的评估一致**：我给 Soundness 3.5/5，其中一个关键扣分点就是 title-evidence mismatch。R1 的 N2 从功效分析角度提供了更详细的论证。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-N3: 诊断框架 100% 准确率可能是 trivial classifier**\n\n这是一个精准的批评。我在自己的 W2 中也指出了 in-sample 问题，但 R1 的 trivial classifier 论证更加尖锐：\n\n1. **基率论证成立**：查看 Figure 4 (p7) 的决策树，所有叶节点的预测结果均为 neutral-to-negative。如果数据中不存在任何正面案例，则 \"always predict no improvement\" 这个零规则分类器同样达到 100% 准确率。\n2. **Held-out 验证不足以反驳**：§5.3 提到在 LLaMA-3.1-8B 和 Qwen3-14B 上做了前瞻性验证，但这两个模型同样产生 negative 结果。真正的验证需要包含 *positive cases*——即存在 SICA 确实改善 SC 的条件。但论文的核心论点恰恰是不存在这样的条件，这构成一个内在矛盾。\n3. **诊断框架的实际价值在于 failure mode 分类而非二分类预测**：Figure 4 区分了退化型（C₀ > 5%）、多样性不足型（K_eff > 1.30）和确认偏差型（SC ≥ 60%）。这种分类对从业者理解 *为什么* 方法失败有指导意义，但 \"100% accuracy\" 的表述暗示的是预测能力，而非解释能力。建议改为 \"correctly characterizes the failure mode\" 而非 \"correctly classifies\"。\n\n---\n\n### Should-Fix 问题\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W1: Corollary 1 贡献定位不清**\n\n同意。我在自己的审稿中将此列为 W1 并给出了相似评价：I(C;Y) ≤ I(T;Y) 是 DPI 在 Markov chain T→C 上的直接应用，几乎不含非显然步骤。论文的贡献在于 *选择了正确的 Markov chain 建模*（modeling contribution），而非推导本身（derivation contribution）。建议论文明确区分这两个层次。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W2: BR 缺少 null model 分解**\n\n这是一个我自己遗漏的重要技术点。\n\n1. **基率效应的量化**：论文 §5.1 (p6) 提到 \"reflecting both the base-rate effect of trace-count distributions and any additional extraction alignment\"，但未提供形式化分解。如果 trace 分布为 60:40（nₐ/nₐ' = 1.5），纯随机约束-答案关联已经产生 BR = 1.5。Table 2 中 Mistral-7B 的 BR = 4.97、Gemini-2.5-Pro 的 BR = 12.19，其中多少是 base-rate 贡献、多少是 excess extraction bias？\n2. **permutation null model 的建议合理**：打乱约束与 trace 的对应关系后计算 null BR，再用 (observed BR - null BR) 作为 excess bias 的度量，可以更清晰地展示提取过程本身引入了多少额外偏差。\n3. **这不影响核心结论但影响机制解释的精度**：即使 excess bias = 0（即 BR 完全由 base-rate 解释），论文的 negative result 仍然成立——约束提取没有 *超越* trace 分布。但作者声称的 \"structural\" bias 需要 excess bias > 0 才能成立。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W3: Cross-architecture escape 结论过度推断**\n\n同意。我在自己的审稿中将跨架构发现列为优势（S4），但未充分关注统计鲁棒性。补充：\n\n1. **Spearman ρ=1.0, n=6 的 p 值问题**：6 个点的完美排名相关虽然 p < 0.05，但鲁棒性极低——移除任一点都可能破坏相关性。且论文 p7-8 Table 3 仅覆盖 FOLIO 和 ProofWriter 两个数据集，不足以建立 \"capability-gap pattern\" 这样的一般性规律。\n2. **但方向性发现仍有价值**：即使证据不足以支撑强因果声明，\"独立验证器可打破 SCB 瓶颈\" 这一方向性发现对后续研究有启示意义。建议将 §6 的措辞从 \"confirming\" 改为 \"suggesting\" 或 \"providing preliminary evidence for\"。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W4: α=0.5 sensitivity 分析实质上确认退化**\n\n同意。查看 Table 11 (Appendix H, p16)，所有 α ∈ {0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 1.0} 下 SICA-SC agreement 均 ≥ 99%。这不是 \"SICA 对 α 鲁棒\"，而是 \"退化条件 |C⁻| ≈ 0 使 α 变得无关\"——当惩罚项的输入（矛盾约束）趋近于零时，惩罚系数的值无论如何都不影响结果。论文应明确承认这一点，而非将其包装为鲁棒性验证。\n\n---\n\n**[REBUT] R1-W5: Multi-seed replication 覆盖率不足**\n\n部分反驳。\n\n1. **Mistral-7B 的 Δ ∈ [-4.41, +3.43] 确实令人担忧**：Table 31 (Appendix X, p24) 显示跨 seed 的不稳定性。但论文 p24 报告 mean Δ = -0.17 ± 2.31 (SD)，95% CI 覆盖零，这本身就是 null effect 的证据。\n2. **关于覆盖率**：论文在 Appendix X.1 (p24) 同时报告了 Qwen2.5-14B 的 multi-seed 结果（mean Δ = +0.75 ± 1.87, CI: [-1.24, +0.36]），同样与 null 一致。两个模型（一个 7B，一个 14B）的 multi-seed 结果一致支持 null 假说。\n3. **但 R1 的方向正确**：更多模型的 multi-seed 数据确实会加强结论。建议作为 revision 中的优先补充实验。\n4. **反驳理由**：将此从 \"should-fix\" 降级为 \"nice-to-have\"——现有两个模型的 multi-seed 数据虽不充分但方向一致，不构成对核心结论的实质性威胁。\n\n---\n\n### Questions 回应\n\n**Q1**: 同意 R1 的关注——score(a) = nₐ 需要从 Eq. 3 严格推导，中间需证明去重后的权重分布不改变答案间的比例关系（见我对 N1 的分析）。\n\n**Q2**: 同意 R1 的质疑。我建议作者回应时展示：(a) 诊断框架相比 trivial classifier 的附加信息（failure mode 分类），以及 (b) 在某些条件下诊断指标接近但未跨越边界时是否存在 false-near-miss 案例。\n\n**Q3**: ±2pp margin 的先验依据确实缺失。建议作者说明选择依据——是基于领域惯例（如 NLP benchmark 的 typical noise floor）还是事后选择？如果是后者，存在 specification searching 风险。\n\n**Q4**: 合理的实验细节问题。§6 的 cross-architecture 实验是否使用了与 §4 相同的 α=0.5 应该在方法描述中明确。\n\n**Q5**: Table 2 (p6) 中 Gemini-2.5-Pro 的 BR=12.19 确实是异常值（其他模型 BR ∈ [1.66, 4.97]）。结合 R1-W2 的 null model 分解，需要检查 Gemini 是否在 FOLIO 上有极端不平衡的 trace-count 分布导致基率 BR 本身很高，还是 extraction 过程引入了异常偏差。论文 p6 脚注提到 Gemini (n_a ≤ 40) 的 CI 更宽，但未解释 BR 异常值的成因。\n\n---\n\n### Missing References 回应\n\n- **Cohen's h**：[AGREE] 作为标准化效应量，Cohen's h 对读者理解 ±2pp 在不同基率下的实际意义有帮助。例如在 SC=50% vs SC=90% 时，2pp 的 Cohen's h 值差异显著。\n- **Burnell et al. 的实际评分机制下不成立。具体分析：\n\n1. **权重与计数的混淆**：§3.1 (p3) 定义 wₖ = \"the number of source traces\"（该去重约束出现在多少条 trace 中），所以 Σ wc ≠ nₐ，而是 Σ wc = 所有来自答案 a 的 (trace, constraint) 对的总数。如果每条 trace 平均生成 m 条约束，则 Σ wc = m · nₐ，是 *比例* 关系而非 *等式*。\n2. **42.9% 去重率的影响**：不同答案组的约束去重率可能不同。如果多数答案组的约束高度重复（高去重率，少数唯一约束但高权重）而少数答案组约束更多样，则 score(a)/score(a') 不再精确等于 nₐ/nₐ'。\n3. **但结论可能仍然成立**：BR ≥ 1 只需要 score(majority answer) ≥ score(any other answer)，这在 \"约束按比例反映 trace 分布\" 的合理假设下仍然成立。问题是 **证明的严谨性**，而非结论的正确性。\n\n**补充**：我在自己的审稿中也指出了 Markov chain 假设 M→T→A→C→S 缺乏显式论证（我的 N2）。R1 的 N1 从评分函数层面、我的 N2 从条件独立性层面分别暴露了 Proposition 1 的不同弱点——两者叠加使该定理的可靠性进一步存疑。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-N2: 统计功效不足覆盖面过大**\n\n完全同意，且与我的 N1（标题 overclaim）一致。补充几点：\n\n1. **GSM8K (n=30) 和 AIME (n=22) 不应出现在主结果中**：这些样本量下，即使效应为 5pp 也检测不到（Table 6, Appendix D）。将它们与 n=204 的 FOLIO 条件并列展示，给读者造成 \"16 个条件都说 no\" 的错误印象。建议将极小样本条件移至 Appendix 作为 exploratory evidence。\n2. **Table 1 的呈现方式加剧问题**：Table 1 用 \"†gold ICL\" 和 \"◊seed-confirmatory\" 诊断框架 100% 准确率可能是 trivial classifier**\n\n这是一个精准的批评。我在自己的 W2 中也指出了 in-sample 问题，但 R1 的 trivial classifier 论证更加尖锐：\n\n1. **基率论证成立**：查看 Figure 4 (p7) 的决策树，所有叶节点的预测结果均为 neutral-to-negative。如果数据中不存在任何正面案例，则 \"always predict no improvement\" 这个零规则分类器同样达到 100% 准确率。\n2. **Held-out 验证不足以反驳**：§5.3 提到在 LLaMA-3.1-8B 和 Qwen3-14B 上做了前瞻性验证，但这两个模型同样产生 negative 结果。真正的验证需要包含 *positive cases*——即存在 SICA 确实改善 SC 的条件。但论文的核心论点恰恰是不存在这样的条件，这构成一个内在矛盾。\n3. **诊断框架的实际价值在于 failure mode 分类而非二分类预测**：Figure 4 区分了退化型（C₀ > 5%）、多样性不足型（K_eff > 1.30）和确认偏差型（SC ≥ 60%）。这种分类对从业者理解 *为什么* 方法失败有指导意义，但 \"100% accuracy\" 的表述暗示的是预测能力，而非解释能力。建议改为 \"correctly characterizes the failure mode\" 而非 \"correctly classifies\"。\n\n---\n\n### Should-Fix 问题\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W1: Corollary 1 贡献定位不清**\n\n同意。我在自己的审稿中将此列为 W1 并给出了相似评价：I(C;Y) ≤ I(T;Y) 是 DPI 在 Markov chain T→C 上的直接应用，几乎不含非显然步骤。论文的贡献在于 *选择了正确的 Markov chain 建模*（modeling contribution），而非推导本身（derivation extraction bias？\n2. **permutation null model 的建议合理**：打乱约束与 trace 的对应关系后计算 null BR，再用 (observed BR - null BR) 作为 excess bias 的度量，可以更清晰地展示提取过程本身引入了多少额外偏差。\n3. **这不影响核心结论但影响机制解释的精度**：即使 excess bias = 0（即 BR 完全由 base-rate 解释），论文的 negative result 仍然成立——约束提取没有 *超越* trace 分布。但作者声称的 \"structural\" bias 需要 excess bias > 0 才能成立。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W3: Cross-architecture escape CI: [-1.24, +0.36]），同样与 null 一致。两个模型（一个 7B，一个 14B）的 multi-seed 结果一致支持 null 假说。\n3. **但 R1 的方向正确**：更多模型的 multi-seed 数据确实会加强结论。建议作为 revision 中的优先补充实验。\n4. **反驳理由**：将此从 \"should-fix\" 降级为 [Round 1 审稿请求]:
你是 Reviewer 2，负责审查**新颖性、完整性和写作质量**。

## 论文信息
- **标题**: Beyond Self-Consistency: Self-Extracted Constraints Do Not Improve LLM Logical Reasoning
- **论文源码**: `projects/symb_invariant_consensus/docs/paper/main.tex`（及同目录下的 abstract.tex, introduction.tex, method.tex, experiments.tex, analysis.tex, solutions.tex, discussion.tex, limitations_content.tex, related_work.tex, appendix.tex）
- **PDF**: `projects/symb_invariant_consensus/artifacts/review_round_15/submitted.pdf`

## Venue 要求（EMNLP 2026 via ARR）
- **截止日期**: 2026-05-25
- **页数限制**: 正文 8 页（严格），参考文献无限，Limitations/Ethics 不计入
- **模板**: ACL template（`\usepackage[review]{acl}`）
- **匿名**: Double-blind，作者列 Anonymous
- **评分标准**: 5=Award, 4.5=Borderline Award, 4=Conference, 3.5=Borderline Conference, 3=Findings, 2.5=Borderline Findings, 2=Resubmit next cycle, 1.5=Resubmit after next cycle, 1=Do not resubmit

## 你的审查侧重

**核心关注**:
1. **SCB (Symbolic Confirmation Bias) 概念新颖性**: 与 Huang et al. (2024) self-correction limitation、Wan et al. (2025) CoT confirmation bias 的关系？是已知现象在 FOL 载体上的实例化，还是有独立的理论/实证贡献？论文是否清晰建立了 distinction？
2. **Contributions 定位**: 三个 contribution（symbolic mirage, diagnostic framework, cross-architecture escape）的力度是否足够 EMNLP main conference？是否存在 scope 震荡（SICA-specific claims 与 general claims 反复切换）？
3. **Related Work 覆盖度**: 是否有重要遗漏？Neuro-symbolic reasoning 分类（specification-generation / program-generation / trace-extraction）是否准确完整？Self-verification 文献是否充分覆盖？是否漏引了 Reflexion (Shinn 2023)、LATS (Zhou 2023) 等重要工作？
4. **写作质量**: Abstract 信息密度是否过高？符号/指标过载（K_eff, BR, C_0, κ 等）？K_eff 缺少直觉解释？"Symbolic Mirage" 作为 Section 标题是否过于 dramatic？
5. **标题与 claims 的匹配**: "Do Not Improve" 在 10/16 conditions underpowered 下是否 overclaiming？"LLM Reasoning" vs 仅覆盖逻辑推理域？
6. **格式合规**: 正文是否严格 ≤8 页？Limitations section 是否在正文且仅一处？匿名规范是否遵守？ACL template 使用是否正确？
7. **27 training-free variants**: scope boundary 是否清楚？是否明确声明未尝试 trained constraint extractor？

**输出格式**: 按 Summary → Strengths → Weaknesses (按严重性排序: Must-Fix / Should-Fix / Nice-to-Have) → Questions → Missing References → Overall Assessment (含评分和置信度) 组织。每个 weakness 标注严重性 [critical/major/minor]。

Based on the search results, here's what I found about ACL ARR 2025-2026 reviewer guidelines and criteria:

## Review Scoring and Criteria

Starting with February 2025, ARR reviewers are asked to provide three scores: soundness, excitement, and overall recommendation. The soundness scores must be justified by the text of the review.

## Reviewer Expectations

All reviewers must familiarize themselves with the latest version of the ARR reviewer guidelines and perform the reviews on time and in line with ARR requirements.

## Reviewing Policies for 2025-2026

Starting in May 2025, ARR adopts a new policy on the 'highly irresponsible' (meta-)reviewers. The program chairs and ARR EiCs will consider egregious cases reported by chairs, including extremely terse reviews, unprofessional tone, and LLM-generated reviews.

_source line 1399 · 2026-05-23T10:13:01.741105+00:00_

# ARR Review Form Scoring Rubric

Based on the provided guidelines, the document describes three main scoring dimensions but **does not provide explicit numerical scales or detailed score definitions**. Here's what is specified:

## Three Scoring Dimensions

1. **Soundness**: "Soundness scores must be justified by the text of the review. If you give a low Soundness score without finding any major faults, this means that your review is not a faithful explanation of your recommendation."

2. **Excitement**: This captures subjective personal interest, described as "orthogonal to the assessment of the soundness of the paper" and reflecting "our personal taste."

3. **Overall Assessment**: "An explicit recommendation for the outcome of this paper, if it were committed to an \*ACL venue. This is a composite score reflecting your assessment of soundness, excitement, and also other factors like novelty and impact."

## Key Scoring Guidance

The guidelines emphasize that papers recommended for Findings and main conferences must be "sufficiently sound and reproducible," though reviewers may find work sound yet personally uninterested, or conversely, be excited about papers with acknowledged limitations.

**Critical Gap**: The document does not specify the actual numerical scale (e.g., 1-5, 1-10) or provide detailed rubrics defining what constitutes each score level. Reviewers are directed to the separate "ARR review form" post for additional details.

_source line 1413 · 2026-05-23T10:13:29.395271+00:00_

# ACL Rolling Review Form - Complete Rubric

## Reviewer Confidence
Scale 1-5:
- **5**: "Positive that my evaluation is correct. I read the paper very carefully and am familiar with related work."
- **4**: "Quite sure. I tried to check the important points carefully."
- **3**: "Pretty sure, but there's a chance I missed something."
- **2**: "Willing to defend my evaluation, but it is fairly likely that I missed some details."
- **1**: "Not my area, or paper is very hard to understand."

## Soundness
Scale 1-5 (with .5 increments):
- **5**: Exceptionally thorough study for its type
- **4**: "This study provides sufficient support for all of its claims."
- **3**: "This study provides sufficient support for its main claims. Some minor points may need extra support."
- **2**: "Some of the main claims are not sufficiently supported. There are major technical/methodological problems."
- **1**: "This study is not yet sufficiently thorough to warrant publication."

## Excitement
Scale 1-5 (with .5 increments):
- **5**: "I would recommend this paper to others and/or attend its presentation."
- **4**: "I would mention this paper to others and/or make an effort to attend."
- **3**: "I might mention some points of this paper to others."
- **2**: "This paper does not resonate with me, but it might with others."
- **1**: "This paper does not resonate with me, and I don't think it would with others."

## Overall Assessment
Scale 1-5 (with .5 increments):
- **5**: Consider for Award (top 2.5%)
- **4.5**: Borderline Award
- **4**: "I think this paper could be accepted to an *ACL conference."
- **3.5**: Borderline Conference
- **3**: "I think this paper could be accepted to the Findings of the ACL."
- **2.5**: Borderline Findings
- **2**: Resubmit next cycle
- **1.5**: Resubmit after next cycle
- **1**: Do not resubmit

## Reproducibility
Scale 1-5:
- **5**: "They could easily reproduce the results."
- **4**: "They could mostly reproduce the results, but there may be some variation."

### Tool result

_source line 1459 · 2026-05-23T10:16:17.956784+00:00_

1	Can symbolic verification via a provably sound solver improve Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning when constraints are self-extracted from the model's own traces? We find no evidence of improvement. We introduce Symbolic Invariant Consensus Aggregation (SICA), which extracts logical constraints from chain-of-thought traces and selects answers via weighted MAX-SAT. Despite Z3 validating 99\% of self-extracted constraints, SICA produces answers identical to majority voting on over 90\% of problems across six models (7B--32B) and three reasoning domains. No condition shows a significant positive effect after FDR control; four of six powered conditions confirm equivalence within $\pm$2\,pp (TOST), though ten conditions remain underpowered for effects below ${\sim}$2\,pp. We systematically diagnose the failure as \emph{symbolic confirmation bias}: self-extracted constraints track the model's majority answer (bias ratios $1.66$--$4.97\times$ across nine models, 7B to frontier). We identify the structural mechanism---under source-attribution scoring and empirical degeneration, SICA reduces to majority vote (Proposition~1)---and the $M{\to}T{\to}A{\to}C$ Markov chain that bounds self-extracted information (Remark~1), providing independent evidence consistent with the self-correction limitations of \citet{huang2024selfcorrect}. Twenty-seven training-free variants within the constraint-extraction paradigm fail due to a persistent independence-accuracy trade-off. Preliminary cross-architecture verification escapes the bias ($+6.67$\,pp on the weakest generator, $p < 0.001$). Three zero-cost diagnostics characterize failure modes across all 60 tested conditions within this paradigm.
2

1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related-work}
3	
4	\looseness=-1
5	\paragraph{Self-consistency and ensemble theory.}
6	Self-consistency (SC) selects the most frequent answer across chain-of-thought traces \citep{wang2023sc}. Extensions address free-form generation \citep{chen2024usc}, confidence weighting \citep{taubenfeld2025cisc}, diverse prompting \citep{li2023diverse}, and reasoning-aware filtering \citep{wan2024rasc, toh2024notallvotes, kim2026reasc}. SC gains plateau early \citep{loo2025reevaluating, byerly2026selfconsistency}. The Condorcet Jury Theorem \citep{condorcet1785} guarantees majority-vote convergence under independence; \citet{ladha1992correlated} show correlated voters reduce effective ensemble size. Our $K_\text{eff}$ adapts Fleiss' $\kappa$ from ensemble diversity theory \citep{kuncheva2003measures, brown2005diversity} to quantify this compression (\S\ref{sec:pipeline}). \citet{kim2025correlated} demonstrate correlated errors in LLM outputs; \citet{kuai2026independent} propose cross-model independence auditing, complementary to our within-model process-$\kappa$.
7	
8	\looseness=-1
9	\paragraph{Neuro-symbolic reasoning.}
10	We distinguish three paradigms: \emph{specification-generation} \citep{pan2023logiclm, olausson2023linc, ye2024satlm} translates problems into formal specifications before answering; \emph{program-generation} \citep{gao2023pal, chen2023program, lyu2023faithful} produces executable code; both formalize \emph{before} answer generation. \emph{Trace-extraction} (this work) operates post-hoc, inheriting confirmation bias (\S\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}). Recent extensions expand these paradigms with symbolic planning, logic agents, and verified reasoning \citep{ryu2025clover, xu2026dylos, li2025aristotle, wang2025fover, singh2026verge, luo2025logicagent, miya2025eidoku}; \citet{amonkar2025realitycheck} show formalization quality degrades with problem complexity; \citet{chan2026position} argue logical soundness alone is unrelia
…[truncated 3435 chars]

1	\section{Introduction}\label{sec:introduction}
2	
3	\looseness=-1
4	Self-Consistency (SC) \citep{wang2023sc} selects the majority answer across $K$ chain-of-thought traces, a strategy grounded in the Condorcet Jury Theorem: independent voters converge to truth \citep{condorcet1785, ladha1992correlated}. In practice, SC's gains plateau for strong models \citep{loo2025reevaluating}, pointing to correlated traces that reduce the effective ensemble size. A natural remedy is to move beyond simple vote-counting: extract logical constraints from the traces and let a provably sound solver adjudicate. If the solver can filter contradictions and enforce logical consistency, it should recover information that raw majority voting discards.
5	
6	\looseness=-1
7	We test this intuition through Symbolic Invariant Consensus Aggregation (SICA), which extracts logical constraints from reasoning traces (prompted as first-order logic; 99\% are propositional at the Z3 level) and selects answers via weighted partial MAX-SAT (\S\ref{sec:pipeline}; Figure~\ref{fig:pipeline}). The result is unequivocal: despite Z3 validating nearly all self-extracted constraints, SICA produces answers identical to majority voting on the vast majority of problems. Across six models (7B--32B), three reasoning domains, and multi-seed replication, no condition shows a significant positive effect after FDR control (Table~\ref{tab:main_results}). Four conditions confirm equivalence within $\pm$2\,pp; ten remain underpowered for effects below ${\sim}$2\,pp.
8	
9	\looseness=-1
10	The failure is not in the solver but in the constraints it operates on. Self-extracted constraints systematically support the model's majority answer, a phenomenon we term \emph{symbolic confirmation bias} (\S\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}). Because each trace determines both an answer and its constraints, the formal channel reduces to a weighted echo of the majority vote. We prove this degeneration is structural (Proposition~1) and show it 
…[truncated 3959 chars]

1	\section{Discussion and Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	\label{sec:conclusion}
4	
5	\looseness=-1
6	Our central finding is that the value of formal verification depends on the \emph{independence} of its inputs, not the soundness of its logic. Three diagnostics ($K_\text{eff}$, BR, $C_0$) operationalize this principle and correctly characterize failure modes across all 60 tested conditions within the self-extraction paradigm, with decision boundaries generalizing prospectively to held-out model families (\S\ref{sec:diagnostic-framework}). The theoretical bounds (Proposition~1, Remark~1) apply to any deterministic self-extraction pipeline by construction; empirical validation covers 27 training-free variants within the SICA paradigm.
7	
8	\looseness=-1
9	This independence principle has direct consequences for test-time compute scaling \citep{snell2025scaling}: with $K_\text{eff} \leq 3.62$ for $K{=}12$, same-model traces scale redundancy, not information. The principle applies to any self-verification method that draws on the generating model's own distribution, providing independent evidence consistent with \citet{huang2024selfcorrect}. Escaping the bottleneck requires structural independence, capability ordering, and task-verifier alignment (\S\ref{sec:cross-arch}); whether trained extractors can shift this boundary remains open. The diagnostics we provide enable future work to recognize this limitation upfront.
10

1	\section{The Symbolic Mirage}
2	\label{sec:analysis}
3	
4	\subsection{Symbolic Confirmation Bias}
5	\label{sec:confirmation-bias}
6	
7	\looseness=-1 Self-extracted constraints systematically align with the model's majority answer. On Mistral-7B FOLIO, when SICA errs, 83.3\% of constraints support the incorrect answer---bias ratio $4.97\times$ (Table~\ref{tab:cross-model-bias})---and 94\% of errors overlap with SC. Z3 rejects only 3.4\% of constraints, and MAX-SAT produces answers identical to count-only aggregation on 94.4\% of problems (Table~\ref{tab:ablation}), confirming that the formal verification layer is vacuous (Eq.~\ref{eq:degenerate}): 99\% of constraints pass Z3 validation yet add no information beyond majority voting. The $\alpha$-insensitivity confirms degeneration: under $|\mathcal{C}^{\setminus}| \approx 0$ the penalty term vanishes and $\alpha$ becomes irrelevant, producing $\geq$99\% agreement across $\alpha \in \{0.1, \ldots, 0.9\}$ as a ceiling effect (Table~\ref{tab:alpha-ablation}).
8	
9	\looseness=-1 Empirically, confirmation bias extends beyond incorrect instances. On problems where SC selects the correct answer, constraints still disproportionately support the majority ($\text{BR}_\text{correct}$ $1.29$--$3.64\times$ across all six tested conditions, with 5/6 showing $\text{BR}_\text{correct} \geq \text{BR}_\text{wrong}$), confirming that self-extracted constraints track distributional frequency rather than logical validity. A permutation null model---randomizing constraint--answer associations while preserving per-trace counts (1{,}000 permutations)---yields $\text{BR}_\text{null} \approx \text{BR}_\text{obs}$ across all tested conditions ($p > 0.7$), confirming that observed BR is fully explained by the trace-count base rate. The bias is structural precisely because it is inherent to the majority-vote geometry (Proposition~1), not an artifact of extraction.
10

1	\section{Escaping the Bottleneck}
2	\label{sec:solutions}
3	\label{sec:cross-arch}
4	
5	\paragraph{Motivation.}
6	\looseness=-1 No training-free self-extraction method achieves independence from the model's distributional prior (\S\ref{sec:remediation}). Remark~1 implies that any escape must break the self-extraction dependency: the information ceiling $I(C;Y) \leq I(T;Y)$ binds whenever constraints are deterministic functions of the generating model's traces. We test whether a structurally independent verifier, sharing neither architecture nor training data with the generator, provides corrective signal that escapes this bound.
7	
8	\paragraph{Method.}
9	\looseness=-1 We use DeBERTa-large \citep{he2021deberta} (406M, encoder-only), fine-tuned on MultiNLI \citep{williams2018mnli}, to classify (premise, conclusion) pairs as entailment, contradiction, or neutral. In combo mode, each NLI-supporting vote counts $w$ times alongside SC's $K$ votes. We evaluate on Mistral-7B FOLIO ($n = 204$) and ProofWriter depth-5 ($n = 600$).
10	
11	\paragraph{Zero-shot results.}
12	\looseness=-1 DeBERTa NLI alone achieves 49.51\% on FOLIO and 55.00\% on ProofWriter. Combo $w{=}3$ yields $+6.67$\,pp on ProofWriter for Mistral-7B ($p < 0.001$, significant after BH correction) and $+2.94$\,pp on FOLIO (n.s.); all generators with SC $\geq 72\%$ show no significant improvement ($\Delta \leq +0.83$\,pp). Same-model SICA \emph{degrades} ProofWriter ($-2.83$\,pp; Table~\ref{tab:main_results}), illustrating that corrective signal must originate outside the model's distribution.
13	
14	\paragraph{Capability gap.}
15	\looseness=-1 Four generators spanning a 47-point SC range on ProofWriter ($n{=}600$), all paired with DeBERTa-large-mnli (55.00\% standalone; Table~\ref{tab:cross_generator_nli}): only Mistral-7B (SC 39.33\%, below the verifier) gains significantly ($+6.67$\,pp, $p < 0.001$); all three above the verifier show no significant improvement ($\Delta \leq +0.83$\,pp). 5	\looseness=-1 Table~\ref{tab:main_results} reports accuracy across all non-degenerate self-extraction conditions sorted by SC baseline. No condition shows a significant SICA effect after FDR control (BH, $m{=}16$). Two conditions show nominally significant degradation: Mistral-7B on ProofWriter ($-2.83$\,pp, raw $p = 0.014$) and Qwen2.5-14B on ProofWriter ($-2.50$\,pp, raw $p = 0.011$); both reach adjusted $p = 0.112$. An aggregate sign test yields 8 positive and 7 negative $\Delta$ values ($p = 1.00$, two-sided). When SICA and SC disagree, SC selects the correct answer $2.6\times$ more often (139 vs.\ 53 across conditions), indicating that constraint-based reweighting introduces noise rather than corrective signal.

(2024) \"LLMs cannot self-correct without external feedback\" 的框架下具有较高的先验可预测性。 |\n| Reproducibility | 4 / 5 | 模型、数据集、超参数（K=12, T=0.7, T_ext=0.3, α=0.5）均有明确说明；Z3 求解器和 MAX-SAT 算法有伪代码（Appendix B）；prompt 模板完整（Appendix A）。缺少：代码/数据未随投稿提供。 |\n\n---\n\n## Must-Fix 问题（影响接收决定）\n\n**N1** [critical] 标题 \"Do Not Improve\" 与统计证据不匹配\n\n- 具体位置：标题；Section 4 (Table 1)；Appendix D (power analysis)\n- 问题：标题断言 \"Self-Extracted Constraints Do Not Improve LLM Logical Reasoning\"，这是一个全称否定命题。但论文自身承认 16 个非退化条件中有 10 个 underpowered（MDE 1.86–4.84 pp，observed power < 21%），只有 4 个条件通过 TOST 等价检验确认 ±2pp 内无效果（Section 4, para 5: \"ten conditions remain underpowered for effects below ~2pp\"）。Abstract 中使用了更审慎的 \"We find no evidence of improvement\"，但标题的 \"Do Not Improve\" 是一个 absence-of-evidence → evidence-of-absence 的逻辑跳跃。\n- 为什么影响接收：这是 soundness 层面的问题。Reviewer 和 AC 对 overclaiming 非常敏感；一个基于 underpowered 数据的全称否定主张会削弱论文的可信度。\n- 建议修改方向：(a) 标题改为更审慎的表述，如 \"…Show No Evidence of Improving…\" 或 \"…Fail to Improve…\"（暗示当前证据下的失败而非逻辑上的不可能）；(b) 或在 abstract 开头更显著地 qualify 这一结论的统计边界。\n\n**N2** [critical] SCB 相对于 Huang et al. (2024) 的增量新颖性论证不足\n\n- 具体位置：Section 2 (\"Distinction from CoT confirmation bias\") 及 Introduction para 3\n- 问题：论文多次引用 Huang et al. (2024) \"LLMs cannot self-correct reasoning without external feedback\"，并将自身定位为提供 \"independent evidence consistent with this limitation in formal verification\"（Introduction para 3）。这个定位有两个风险：(1) 如果 SCB 只是 Huang et al. 的一般性结论在 FOL/Z3 载体上的实例化，则贡献降级为 case study；(2) Section 2 中对 CoT confirmation bias (Wan et al. 2025, Turpin et al. 2024) 的区分——\"trace level vs. aggregation level\"——虽然在概念上成立，但论文没有提供实证证据证明这两种 bias 是可分离的（例如，控制 trace faithfulness 后 SCB 是否仍然存在）。\n- 为什么影响接收：新颖性是 EMNLP main conference 的核心维度。如果 AC 判定 SCB 只是已知现象的重新包装，论文会被降级到 Findings。\n- 建议修改方向：(a) 明确陈述 SCB 提供了什么 Huang et al. (2024) 没有的——例如 Proposition 1 的形式化、K_eff/BR/C_0 诊断框架、27 种变体的系统排除——这些不是 \"consistent with\" 而是 \"extends\"；(b) 最好增加一个实验，在控制 trace faithfulness 的条件下测试 aggregation-level bias 是否独立存在。\n\n---\n\n## Should-Fix 问题（影响评分但不一票否决）\n\n**W1** [major] Scope 震荡：SICA-specific claims 与 general claims 反复切换\n\n- 具体位置：Introduction contributions list vs. Discussion (Section 7) vs. 标题\n- 问题：论文在两个 scope 层级之间来回切换。标题说 \"Self-Extracted Constraints Do Not Improve LLM Logical Reasoning\"（通用主张）；Contribution 1 说 \"Provably sound Z3 verification becomes vacuous under self-extraction\"（SICA-specific）；Discussion 说 \"The principle applies to any self-verification method that draws on the generating model's own distribution\"（通用）；但 Proposition 1 的假设条件（degeneration、source-attribution scoring、uniform yield）是 SICA 的具体设计选择，不是所有 self-extraction pipeline 的共性。Remark 1 的 Markov chain 论证更通用，但需要 \"trace-determined constraints\" 这一假设，对于非 deterministic extraction（如 sampling-based constraint generation）不成立。\n- 建议：在 Introduction 和 Discussion 中明确区分哪些结论是 SICA-specific（Proposition 1）、哪些是结构性的（Remark 1 的 information ceiling）、哪些需要额外假设才能推广。\n\n**W2** [major] \"LLM Logical Reasoning\" 标题措辞与实际覆盖范围不匹配\n\n- 具体位置：标题；Section 3.2 (Datasets)\n- 问题：标题说 \"LLM Logical Reasoning\"，但实际测试涵盖 FOLIO/ProofWriter（一阶逻辑）、LogiQA（阅读理解）、StrategyQA（常识推理）、ARC-Challenge（科学推理）、GSM8K（数学推理）。其中 StrategyQA 和 ARC-Challenge 的 constraint extraction 使用的是不同的 prompt（Appendix A: EXTRACTION_PROMPT vs. LOGIC_EXTRACTION_PROMPT），提取的不是 FOL 而是自然语言约束。这既 (a) 比标题暗示的更广（不止逻辑推理），又 (b) 模糊了核心发现的边界（FOL 约束 vs. 自然语言约束的 SCB 机制可能不同）。\n- 建议：标题改为 \"LLM Reasoning\" 或在 Method 中更明确区分 formal (FOL) 和 informal (NL) constraint extraction 的结果差异。\n\n**W3** [major] Cross-architecture escape (Section 6) 贡献过于初步\n\n- 具体位置：Section 6；Table 3\n- 问题：(1) Zero-shot DeBERTa 仅在最弱 generator (Mistral-7B, SC=39.33%) 上显著，其余三个 generator 均不显著——这本质上是 \"用更强模型替代弱模型\" 而非 \"独立验证提供互补信号\"；(2) Domain-adapted verifier (fine-tuned DeBERTa) 效果更好，但这引入了 training，与论文 \"training-free\" 的主线矛盾；(3) 整个 escape 只在 ProofWriter 一个数据集上展示，FOLIO 上不显著。作为三个 contribution 之一，evidence base 太薄。\n- 建议：(a) 降低 cross-architecture escape 在 contribution list 中的地位（从 \"contribution\" 降为 \"preliminary finding\"）；(b) 或增加更多 verifier-generator 组合和数据集的结果。\n\n**W4** [moderate] Abstract 信息过载\n\n- 具体位置：Abstract（1 段，约 200 词）\n- 问题：Abstract 在一段中塞入了 K_eff、BR、C_0、κ、TOST、FDR、Proposition 1、M→T→A→C Markov chain、±2pp、1.66–4.97×、+6.67pp 等大量符号和数字。对于不熟悉该领域的读者，这个信息密度过高。特别是 K_eff 在 abstract 中出现但没有任何直觉解释（读者需要翻到 Section 3 才能理解它是有效集成大小）。\n- 建议：Abstract 分两段，第一段讲 what and why（problem + main finding），第二段讲 how（方法亮点 + 实验规模）。移除 abstract 中的 K_eff/BR 等符号，用自然语言描述。\n\n**W5** [moderate] K_eff 缺少直觉解释\n\n- 具体位置：Section 3 (page 3, Eq. 1)；首次出现在 Abstract\n- 问题：K_eff = K / [1 + (K-1)·κ] 的公式给出了，但论文没有用一句话解释其直觉意义。读者需要理解 Fleiss' κ、ensemble diversity theory 和 Condorcet Jury Theorem 的背景才能理解为什么 K_eff ≤ 1.30 意味着 \"insufficient vote diversity\"。实际上 K_eff 的含义非常直觉：它是 \"K 个 traces 中有多少个等效的独立投票者\"，但论文从未这样说。\n- 建议：在 Eq. 1 后加一句直觉解释，例如 \"Intuitively, K_eff measures how many effectively independent voters K correlated traces are worth.\"\n\n**W6** [minor] \"Symbolic Mirage\" 作为 Section 5 标题稍显 dramatic\n\n- 具体位置：Section 5 标题\n- 问题：学术论文通常偏好描述性标题。\"The Symbolic Mirage\" 虽然准确捕捉了内容（符号验证的表面力量是幻觉），但在 EMNLP 正文中可能被视为过于修辞化。\n- 建议：可改为更中性的 \"Analysis of Symbolic Confirmation Bias\" 或保留现有标题但确保评审人不会因此对论文的严肃性产生疑虑。这是极小的风格问题。\n\n---\n\n## 小问题（不影响接收）\n\n- Section 3.2: GSM8K 仅有 n=200，且被标记为 degenerate (C_0 < 5%)，这意味着 SICA 在数学领域的适用性几乎未被有效测试。可在 Limitations 中提及。\n- Table 2 (page 6): 列出了 9 个模型的 SCB 数据，但 Gemini-2.5-Pro 标注为 interim (n=95/204)，且 n_err=12，置信区间很宽。建议加注 caveat 或移至 appendix。\n- Appendix 体量（16 页）远超正文（8 页）。虽然信息量丰富，但部分内容（如 Section F 的 discordant case studies、Section G 的 oracle control）对审稿判断有较高价值，建议在正文中增加指引。\n- `\\enlargethispage{4\\baselineskip}` 和多处 `\\vspace{-Xmm}` 表明页面预算非常紧张。内容本身没有溢出，但建议检查最终 camera-ready 是否会因模板更新而超页。\n- Limitations section 仅 3 行（约 50 词），虽然 EMNLP 2026 规则说 \"一小段，覆盖 3 个点左右\"，但当前版本几乎是最低限度。可适当展开 statistical power 限制和 English-only 范围。\n\n---\n\n## 主要优势\n\n1. **实验覆盖极其充分**：6 个 open-source 模型 + 3 个 frontier API 模型、7 个数据集、3 个推理域、27 个 training-free 变体、多种子复制——这是我见过的最彻底的 negative-result 论文之一。\n2. **统计方法论严谨**：FDR 控制、TOST 等价检验、power analysis、bootstrap CI、permutation null model、McNemar paired test，远超该领域的平均水准。论文坦诚报告 underpowered conditions（Section 4 para 5），这种透明度值得肯定。\n3. **理论贡献有独立价值**：Proposition 1（argmax preservation under degeneration）和 Remark 1（information ceiling via data processing inequality）为理解 self-extraction pipeline 的局限性提供了结构性框架，可推广到 SICA 之外。\n4. **诊断框架具有实践价值**：C_0、K_eff、BR 三个零成本指标 + 决策边界 + 前瞻验证（held-out model families），使从业者能在 pilot run 阶段判断是否值得部署 self-extraction 方法。\n5. **Related Work 覆盖全面**：结构清晰的分类（specification-generation / program-generation / trace-extraction），Reflexion、LATS、CRITIC 等重要工作均已引用。与 Huang et al. (2024) 和 Wan et al. (2025) 的区分在概念上成立。\n\n---\n\n## 缺失引用\n\n主要引用基本完整。以下是可考虑补充的：\n\n- **Self-Evaluation Guided Beam Search** (Xie et al., 2023): 与 self-verification 相关的 beam search 方法\n- **Verification-guided reasoning** 方向的更多最新工作（2025-2026），如果有的话\n- 建议确认 Zhou et al. (2023/2024) LATS 的引用年份一致性（bib 中标注 2023 但 main.aux 中解析为 2024）\n\n---\n\n## Overall Assessment\n\n**评分: 3.5 / 5 (Borderline Conference)**\n\n这是一篇执行质量很高的负面结果论文，实验设计和统计方法论远超同类工作的平均水准。核心贡献——识别并形式化 Symbolic Confirmation Bias——在概念上有独立价值，诊断框架具有实践意义。\n\n降低评分的主要因素：(1) 标题 overclaiming（全称否定 vs. underpowered evidence）是 soundness 层面的瑕疵；(2) 核心发现与 Huang et al. (2024) 的增量距离不够大，需要更强的 distinction 论证；(3) Cross-architecture escape 作为三个 contribution 之一，evidence base 不足。\n\n如果作者修正标题表述、加强 SCB 的独立新颖性论证、并将 cross-architecture escape 定位为 preliminary finding 而非 main contribution，本文适合 EMNLP main conference。当前状态下，Findings 是更安全的归宿。",
  "reviewer_id": "2",
  "project": "symb_invariant_consensus"
}
```

measures how many effectively independent voters K correlated traces are worth.\"\n\n**W6** [minor] \"Symbolic Mirage\" 作为 Section 5 标题稍显 dramatic\n\n- 具体位置：Section 5 标题\n- 问题：学术论文通常偏好描述性标题。\"The Symbolic Mirage\" 虽然准确捕捉了内容（符号验证的表面力量是幻觉），但在 EMNLP 正文中可能被视为过于修辞化。\n- 建议：可改为更中性的 \"Analysis of Symbolic Confirmation Bias\" 或保留现有标题但确保评审人不会因此对论文的严肃性产生疑虑。这是极小的风格问题。\n\n---\n\n## 小问题（不影响接收）\n\n- Section 3.2: GSM8K 仅有 n=200，且被标记为 degenerate (C_0 < 5%)，这意味着 SICA 在数学领域的适用性几乎未被有效测试。可在 Limitations 中提及。\n- Table 2 (page 6): 列出了 9 个模型的 SCB 数据，但 Gemini-2.5-Pro 标注为 interim (n=95/204)，且 n_err=12，置信区间很宽。建议加注 caveat 或移至 appendix。\n- Appendix 体量（16 页）远超正文（8 页）。虽然信息量丰富，但部分内容（如 Section F 的 discordant case studies、Section G 的 oracle control）对审稿判断有较高价值，建议在正文中增加指引。\n- `\\enlargethispage{4\\baselineskip}` 和多处 `\\vspace{-Xmm}` 表明页面预算非常紧张。内容本身没有溢出，但建议检查最终 camera-ready 是否会因模板更新而超页。\n- Limitations section 仅 3 行（约 50 词），虽然 EMNLP 2026 规则说 \"一小段，覆盖 3 个点左右\"，但当前版本几乎是最低限度。可适当展开 statistical power 限制和 English-only 范围。\n\n---\n\n## 主要优势\n\n1. **实验覆盖极其充分**：6 个 open-source 模型 + 3 个 frontier API 模型、7 个数据集、3 个推理域、27 个 training-free 变体、多种子复制——这是我见过的最彻底的 negative-result 论文之一。\n2. **统计方法论严谨**：FDR 控制、TOST 等价检验、power analysis、bootstrap CI、permutation null model、McNemar paired test，远超该领域的平均水准。论文坦诚报告 underpowered conditions（Section 4 para 5），这种透明度值得肯定。\n3. **理论贡献有独立价值**：Proposition 1（argmax preservation under degeneration）和 Remark 1（information ceiling via data processing inequality）为理解 self-extraction pipeline 的局限性提供了结构性框架，可推广到 SICA 之外。\n4. **诊断框架具有实践价值**：C_0、K_eff、BR 三个零成本指标 + 决策边界 + 前瞻验证（held-out model families），使从业者能在 pilot run 阶段判断是否值得部署 self-extraction 方法。\n5. **Related Work 覆盖全面**：结构清晰的分类（specification-generation / program-generation / trace-extraction），Reflexion、LATS、CRITIC 等重要工作均已引用。与 Huang et al. (2024) 和 Wan et al. (2025) 的区分在概念上成立。\n\n---\n\n## 缺失引用\n\n主要引用基本完整。以下是可考虑补充的：\n\n- **Self-Evaluation Guided Beam Search** (Xie et al., 2023): 与 self-verification 相关的 beam search 方法\n- **Verification-guided reasoning** 方向的更多最新工作（2025-2026），如果有的话\n- 建议确认 Zhou et al. (2023/2024) LATS 的引用年份一致性（bib 中标注 2023 但 main.aux 中解析为 2024）\n\n---\n\n## Overall Assessment\n\n**评分: 3.5 / 5 (Borderline Conference)**\n\n这是一篇执行质量很高的负面结果论文，实验设计和统计方法论远超同类工作的平均水准。核心贡献——识别并形式化 Symbolic Confirmation Bias——在概念上有独立价值，诊断框架具有实践意义。\n\n降低评分的主要因素：(1) 标题 overclaiming（全称否定 vs. underpowered evidence）是 soundness 层面的瑕疵；(2) 核心发现与 Huang et al. (2024) 的增量距离不够大，需要更强的 distinction 论证；(3) Cross-architecture escape 作为三个 contribution 之一，evidence base 不足。\n\n如果作者修正标题表述、加强 SCB 的独立新颖性论证、并将 cross-architecture escape 定位为 preliminary finding 而非 main contribution，本文适合 EMNLP main conference。当前状态下，Findings 是更安全的归宿。",
    "reviewer_id": "2",
    "project": "symb_invariant_consensus"
  }
}

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
以下是 Reviewer 1 的完整审稿意见。请逐条回应：[AGREE] 同意并说明理由，或 [REBUT] 反驳并提供证据（cite 具体 Section/Table/Figure）。

---

## Reviewer 1 审稿报告：方法严谨性与实验设计

**Overall Assessment**: 3.5 / 5 (Borderline Conference)
**Confidence**: 4 / 5

### Must-Fix

**N1** [critical] Proposition 1 的形式化不够严谨，更像是 empirical observation 而非 theorem
- 三个假设中，(i) 使用 |C^∖| ≈ 0 而非精确条件，(iii) "approximately uniform constraint yield" 从未给出数学定义，证明中全程使用 ≈ 而非 =。结论通过 >90% 经验一致性支撑而非严格推出。假设 (iii) 未直接验证。
- 建议：给假设 (iii) 精确数学表述（如 CV < δ），推导近似误差上界；或降格为 Empirical Observation。

**N2** [critical] 标题/中心 claim 与统计检验力不匹配
- 标题 "Do Not Improve" 但 10/16 conditions underpowered (MDE 1.86–4.84pp)。论文自己承认 "Absence of evidence is not evidence of absence"，但标题未反映。
- 建议：标题改为 "Provide No Detectable Improvement" 或加入效应大小限定。

### Should-Fix

**W1** [major] Remark 1 (DPI) 接近 trivial——C = f(T) 的 Markov chain 对任何确定性后处理成立。更有趣的 C ⊥ Y | A 声明未被形式化证明。

**W2** [major] BR 指标的循环性——source-attribution 下 BR > 1 几乎 tautological。56.4% 零变量重叠使约束根本无法跨答案判别。建议报告 BR_excess = BR_obs - BR_null。

**W3** [major] Cross-architecture escape Spearman ρ=1.0 基于 n=6，monotonicity 由 Mistral-7B 单个 outlier 驱动。Table 3 中 3 个 generator 的 Δ 在 0.50–0.83pp 几乎无差异。

**W4** [moderate] TOST ±2pp margin 选择依据不充分——FOLIO 是近二分类，1pp/class 阈值不适用。

### Minor
- M1: Z3 3.4% rejection rate 应更早指出 vacuous
- M2: Mistral-7B 3s vs single-seed 关系需澄清
- M3: 56.4% 零重叠的计算粒度需说明
- M4: Appendix Y 的 evaluation framework 讨论应在主文指引

### Questions
Q1: Proposition 1 假设 (iii) 的约束数量分布？
Q2: 43.6% 有重叠子集的 Z3 拒绝率和 SICA 表现？
Q3: FOLIO 上的 cross-architecture verification 结果？
Q4: 是否尝试过 prompt 层面打破 source-attribution？
Q5: 为什么不默认 α=0？

source-attribution），但论文没有将两者明确连接。建议作者在 Remark 1 中显式声明：在 Proposition 1 的条件下，更强的 I(C;Y) ≤ I(A;Y) 成立。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W2: BR 指标的循环性**\n\n这是 R1 审稿中最 insightful 的观察之一，我在初审中未充分挖掘。\n\n论文自身提供了关键证据：Section 5.1 的度量\" 变为 \"degeneration 的度量\"）。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W3: Cross-architecture Spearman ρ=1.0 基于 n=6**\n\n同意。Table 3 (Section 6) 的数据结构是：Mistral-7B 获得 +6.67pp，其余三个 generator（LLaMA-3.1-8B +0.83pp, Qwen2.5-14B +0.67pp, Qwen3-14B +0.50pp）几乎无差异。所谓的 \"monotonic decrease\" 实质上是一个 outlier（Mistral-7B，唯一 SC 低于 verifier standalone accuracy 的 generator）驱动的二元模式，而非连续的单调关系。\n\nn=6 的 Spearman ρ=1.0 在统计上确实可以达到 正文中更明确地区分二分类 vs. 三分类数据集的 margin 适用性，而非笼统地使用同一 margin。\n\n---\n\n### Minor\n\n**[AGREE] R1-M1**: Z3 3.4% rejection rate 是 SCB 的一个关键前提条件（对应 Proposition 1 假设 (i)），确实应在 Method 或 Results 开头就明确指出，而非等到 Section 5.1。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-M2**: Table 1 中 Mistral-7B 出现多行（s123, s456, s789, 3s），行间关系不直观。建议在 Table caption 或脚注中说明 3s 是三个 seed 的平均。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-M3**: 56.4% 零重叠的计算粒度确实需要说明——是 per-problem、per-trace uniform constraint yield\" 确实没有数学定义（如 coefficient of variation < δ）。证明中 \"By (iii), total weight for answer a is proportional to n_a\" 是一个 hand-wave 步骤——如果 yield 不均匀，这个 proportionality 会有多大误差？论文没有给出近似误差上界。\n\n补充：论文用 >90% 的 SICA-SC 一致率作为经验支撑（Section 5.1: \"SICA produces answers identical to count-only aggregation on 94.4% of problems\"），但这只证明结论大致成立，不能替代严格推导。R1 建议降格为 Empirical Observation 或给出误差界，我认为前者更实际——给 (iii) 一个精确的充分条件 + 误差界会是更好的选择，但即使降格为 Empirical Observation，配合 Remark 1 的 DPI 论证，核心论点仍然成立。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-N2: 标题与统计检验力不匹配**\n\n完全同意，这与我的 N1 一致。标题 \"Do Not Improve\" 是全称否定，但 10/16 条件 underpowered（Appendix D: MDE 1.86–4.84pp, observed power < 21%）。论文自身在 Section 4 para 5 明确承认 \"Absence of evidence is not evidence of absence for small effects\"，这与标题矛盾。R1 建议的 \"Provide No Detectable Improvement\" 比我建议的 \"Show No Evidence of Improving\" 更精确，我支持 R1 的表述。\n\n---\n\n### Should-Fix\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W1: Remark 1 (DPI) 接近 trivial**\n\n同意 R1 的判断。I(C_{1:K}; Y) ≤ I(T_{1:K}; Y) 对任何确定性后处理 C = f(T) 都成立——这不是 self-extraction 的特殊性质，而是数据处理不等式的直接推论。论文真正有价值的 claim 是 C ⊥ Y | A（Figure 3 causal DAG caption 中标注），即约束不含超出答案的额外信息——但这个更强的命题没有被形式化证明。\n\n补充我的观察：论文在 Remark 1 和 Figure 3 之间存在微妙的 claim 升级。Remark 1 只证明了 I(C;Y) ≤ I(T;Y)（trivial），但 Figure 3 的 DAG 隐含了更强的 C ⊥ Y | A（non-trivial）。后者需要证明 T → A 的映射使得 C 中关于 Y 的信息完全被 A 中介——这需要额外的条件（如 Proposition 1 的 degeneration + source-attribution），但论文没有将两者明确连接。建议作者在 Remark 1 中显式声明：在 Proposition 1 的条件下，更强的 I(C;Y) ≤ I(A;Y) 成立。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W2: BR 指标的循环性**\n\n这是 R1 审稿中最 insightful 的观察之一，我在初审中未充分挖掘。\n\n论文自身提供了关键证据：Section 5.1 para 2 报告 permutation null model 产生 BR_null ≈ BR_obs（p > 0.7），并称这 \"confirming that observed BR is fully explained by the trace-count base rate\"。但这恰恰证明了 R1 的论点——如果 BR 完全由 trace-count base rate 解释，那么 BR > 1 不是 \"symbolic confirmation bias\" 的证据，而只是 source-attribution scoring 的数学必然。\n\n56.4% 零变量重叠（Section 5.1 para 3）进一步强化了这个论点：当超过半数的约束在变量层面就无法跨答案比较时，BR > 1 是 by construction 的。\n\nR1 建议报告 BR_excess = BR_obs - BR_null 很有建设性。如果 BR_excess ≈ 0（论文的 permutation test 暗示如此），这实际上意味着 SCB 的 \"bias\" 完全来自结构性的 source-attribution，而非约束内容本身的偏向——这反而强化了 Proposition 1 的 degeneration 论点，但需要重新框定 BR 的角色（从 \"bias 的度量\" 变为 \"degeneration 的度量\"）。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W3: Cross-architecture Spearman ρ=1.0 基于 n=6**\n\n同意。Table 3 (Section 6) 的数据结构是：Mistral-7B 获得 +6.67pp，其余三个 generator（LLaMA-3.1-8B +0.83pp, Qwen2.5-14B +0.67pp, Qwen3-14B +0.50pp）几乎无差异。所谓的 \"monotonic decrease\" 实质上是一个 outlier（Mistral-7B，唯一 SC 低于 verifier standalone accuracy 的 generator）驱动的二元模式，而非连续的单调关系。\n\nn=6 的 Spearman ρ=1.0 在统计上确实可以达到 p=0.003（排列检验），但 R1 指出的 outlier 驱动问题使得这个统计显著性的实质意义大打折扣。更诚实的表述是：\"只有当 generator 弱于 verifier 时才观察到显著增益\"，这是一个二元判断而非连续的 capability-gap pattern。\n\n**[REBUT] R1-W4: TOST ±2pp margin 选择**\n\n部分不同意 R1 的判断。\n\nR1 称 \"FOLIO 是近二分类，1pp/class 阈值不适用\"。但 FOLIO 有三个类别（True/False/Unknown），并非二分类。论文在 Section 4 para 5 给出了 margin 的依据：\"effects below one percentage point per class provide negligible practical benefit on datasets with ≥3 answer categories\"。对于三分类问题，±2pp 总体效应 ≈ ±0.67pp/class，这个阈值确实很小。\n\n不过，R1 的关注有一定道理：(a) ProofWriter 是二分类（True/False），±2pp = ±1pp/class，在 n=600 时这是合理的 margin；(b) 论文在 Appendix 中做了 ±1pp 和 ±3pp 的 sensitivity analysis，结论随 margin 变化（Section 4 para 5 提到 \"equivalence conclusions are margin-dependent\"），这恰恰说明 margin 选择确实影响结论。\n\n总结：±2pp margin 在三分类数据集上合理，但论文应在 Section 4 正文中更明确地区分二分类 vs. 三分类数据集的 margin 适用性，而非笼统地使用同一 margin。\n\n---\n\n### Minor\n\n**[AGREE] R1-M1**: Z3 3.4% rejection rate 是 SCB 的一个关键前提条件（对应 Proposition 1 假设 (i)），确实应在 Method 或 Results 开头就明确指出，而非等到 Section 5.1。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-M2**: Table 1 中 Mistral-7B 出现多行（s123, s456, s789, 3s），行间关系不直观。建议在 Table caption 或脚注中说明 3s 是三个 seed 的平均。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-M3**: 56.4% 零重叠的计算粒度确实需要说明——是 per-problem、per-trace 还是 per-constraint pair。\n\n**[AGREE] R1-M4**: Appendix Y 的 SCB evaluation framework 讨论在 Discussion 中确实缺少指引。\n\n---\n\n### Questions 评论\n\nR1 的五个问题质量很高，我特别关注：\n\n- **Q1 (假设 iii 的约束数量分布)** 和 **Q2 (43.6% 有重叠子集的表现)** 直接指向 Proposition 1 的薄弱环节——如果作者能提供这些数据，将显著加强或削弱该命题的可信度。\n- **Q4 (prompt 层面打破 source-attribution)** 很有启发性——如果可以通过 prompt 设计使约束不再绑定到 source trace，理论上可以打破 Proposition 1 的假设 (ii)，这是 27 种变体中似乎未探索的方向。\n- **Q5 (为什么不默认 α=0)** 指向一个实际的设计选择问题——论文在 Section 5.1 提到 α-insensitivity（α ∈ {0.1,...,0.9} 产生 ≥99% 一致），但没有解释为什么 α=0.5 是默认值而非 α=0（完全忽略 penalty）。如果 degeneration 确实成立，α 的值理论上不应该影响结果。\n\n---\n\n### 综合评价\n\nR1 和我（R2）在以下问题上达成一致：\n1. **标题 overclaiming**（R1-N2 = R2-N1）：最高优先级修改项\n2. **Proposition 1 形式化不足**（R1-N1）：需要降格或加强\n3. **Cross-architecture escape 证据不足**（R1-W3 ≈ R2-W3）\n\nR1 提出了我初审未充分覆盖的重要问题：\n- **BR 循环性**（R1-W2）：这实际上可能需要重新框定 BR 在论文中的角色\n- **Remark 1 的 trivial 性**（R1-W1）：需要将 DPI bound 与 C ⊥ Y | A 的更强命题显式连接\n\n我在一个问题上与 R1 有分歧：\n- **TOST margin**（R1-W4）：±2pp [Round 1 审稿请求]:
你是 Reviewer 2，负责**新颖性与完整性**审查。

## Venue 信息
- **目标会议**：EMNLP 2026（通过 ARR May 2026 cycle 投稿）
- **截止日期**：2026-05-25 AoE
- **格式要求**：Long paper，正文 8 页限制，参考文献不限页数，Limitations section 不计入页数（必须有），ACL style template `[review]` 模式，double-blind
- **评分标准**：5=Consider for Award, 4.5=Borderline Award, 4=Conference, 3.5=Borderline Conference, 3=Findings, 2.5=Borderline Findings, 2=Resubmit next cycle, 1.5=Resubmit after next cycle, 1=Do not resubmit

## 论文路径
- LaTeX: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_3/projects/symb_invariant_consensus/docs/paper/main.tex`
- PDF: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_3/projects/symb_invariant_consensus/artifacts/review_round_16/submitted.pdf`
（main.tex 使用 \input 引入各章节文件，均在同一目录下）

## 你的审查重点

**1. 贡献新颖度**
- "negative result"（自提取约束无效）的发现是否真正令人惊讶？还是 obvious in hindsight？
- Symbolic confirmation bias 概念是否是对 Huang et al. (2024) "LLMs cannot self-correct" 的 trivial extension？论文声称 advances beyond 但实际增量在哪？
- 三个诊断指标 (K_eff, BR, C0) 是否有足够的 practical value？
- Cross-architecture escape 作为贡献是否太 preliminary（仅一个 verifier family，n=6 conditions）？
- 对 NLP/ML 社区的影响和启示是什么？

**2. Related Work 覆盖**
- 检查是否遗漏了重要的 neuro-symbolic reasoning 工作
- 检查 self-correction / self-refinement 文献是否充分
- 检查 test-time compute scaling 相关工作是否覆盖
- 检查 ensemble diversity / committee learning 文献
- 检查引用是否真实存在（特别是 2025-2026 年的引用，如 kim2026reasc, byerly2026selfconsistency, kuai2026independent, xu2026dylos, singh2026verge, jhaveri2026failing, chan2026position, dupo2025 等）
- 是否有 self-citation 暴露匿名性的问题？

**3. 叙事结构与写作质量**
- Abstract 是否在 200 words 以内？（EMNLP 2026 硬约束）
- 论文的叙事弧是否清晰？从 motivation → method → negative result → diagnosis → escape
- 术语一致性：SICA、SC、SCB、BR 等缩写是否首次使用时定义？
- Figure/Table 数量和质量是否恰当？
- 论文节奏是否合理？正文 8 页是否装得下所有必要内容？是否有内容应该移到 appendix？
- Limitations section 是否满足要求（简短、3 个点左右）？

**4. Claims 支撑充分性**
- "no detectable improvement" — 是否 fairly stated given underpowered tests？
- "symbolic confirmation bias" 是否过度命名（给一个 expected behavior 起了一个 dramatic name）？
- "27 training-free variants all fail" — 是否穷尽了所有合理方向？
- "three zero-cost diagnostics" — prospective validation 是否足够？
- Frontier model 实验（Table 3）中部分模型 n_err 很小（如 Gemini n=12），是否过度解读？

**5. 格式合规性**
- 正文是否在 8 页以内？
- Limitations section 是否在正文中（非 appendix）？
- 是否有 double-blind 违规？
- 引用格式是否一致？

请提供完整审稿报告，包含：Summary、Strengths、Weaknesses（按 Must-Fix / Should-Fix / Minor 分级）、Questions、Missing References、Overall Assessment（评分 + 置信度）。

# ACL Rolling Review Form: Complete Scoring Dimensions

## 1. **Reviewer Confidence**
Scale: 1-5
- **5**: "Positive that my evaluation is correct. I read the paper very carefully and am familiar with related work."
- **4**: "Quite sure. I tried to check the important points carefully."
- **3**: "Pretty sure, but there's a chance I missed something."
- **2**: "Willing to defend my evaluation, but it is fairly likely that I missed some details."
- **1**: "Not my area, or paper is very hard to understand."

## 2. **Soundness**
Scale: 1-5 (with .5 increments)
- **5**: Exceptionally thorough study for its type
- **4**: Sufficient support for claims; extra experiments optional
- **3**: Adequate support for main claims; minor details need work
- **2**: Major claims lack sufficient support; technical problems present
- **1**: "Not yet sufficiently thorough to warrant publication or is not relevant to ACL"

## 3. **Excitement**
Scale: 1-5 (with .5 increments)
- **5**: "Highly Exciting: I would recommend this paper to others and/or attend its presentation"
- **4**: Would mention to others; effort to attend presentation
- **3**: Might mention points; attend if time permits
- **2**: Doesn't resonate personally but may interest others
- **1**: "Does not resonate with me, and I don't think it would with others"

## 4. **Overall Assessment**
Scale: 1-5 (with .5 increments)
- **5**: "Consider for Award" (top 2.5% papers)
- **4**: Conference acceptance
- **3**: Findings acceptance
- **2**: Resubmit next cycle
- **1**: "Do not resubmit"

## 5. **Reproducibility**
Scale: 1-5
- **5**: Results easily reproducible
- **4**: Mostly reproducible; minor variations possible
- **3**: Reproducible with difficulty; underspecified parameters
- **2**: Hard to reproduce; unavailable data or insufficient details
- **1**: Results cannot be reproduced

## 6. **Datasets** (if applicable)
Scale: 1-5
- **5**: Enabling new research directions
- **4**: Useful for ongoing work
- **3**: Potentially useful
-

_source line 1555 · 2026-05-23T13:48:16.354945+00:00_

1	Can symbolic verification via a provably sound solver improve Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning when constraints are self-extracted from the model's own traces? We find no evidence of improvement. We introduce Symbolic Invariant Consensus Aggregation (SICA), which extracts logical constraints from chain-of-thought traces and selects answers via weighted MAX-SAT. Despite Z3 validating 99\% of self-extracted constraints, SICA produces answers identical to majority voting on over 90\% of problems across six models (7B--32B) and three reasoning domains. No condition shows a significant positive effect after FDR control; four of six powered conditions confirm equivalence within $\pm$2\,pp (TOST), though ten conditions remain underpowered for effects below ${\sim}$2\,pp. We systematically diagnose the failure as \emph{symbolic confirmation bias}: self-extracted constraints track the model's majority answer (bias ratios $1.66$--$4.97\times$ across nine models, 7B to frontier). We identify the structural mechanism---under source-attribution scoring and empirical degeneration, SICA reduces to majority vote (Observation~1)---and the $M{\to}T{\to}A{\to}C$ Markov chain that bounds self-extracted information (Remark~1), advancing beyond \citet{huang2024selfcorrect}'s findings to formal self-verification. Twenty-seven training-free variants within the constraint-extraction paradigm fail due to a persistent independence-accuracy trade-off. Preliminary cross-architecture verification escapes the bias ($+6.67$\,pp on the weakest generator, $p < 0.001$). Three zero-cost diagnostics characterize failure modes across all 60 tested conditions within this paradigm.
2

Based on the search results, I found information about the research you're looking for:

The paper "Adaptive LLM-Symbolic Reasoning via Dynamic Logical Solver Composition" was authored by Lei Xu, Pierre Beckmann, Marco Valentino, and Andre Freitas and presented at EACL 2026.

The research introduces an adaptive, multi-paradigm, neuro-symbolic inference framework that automatically identifies formal reasoning strategies from problems expressed in natural language and dynamically selects and applies specialized formal logical solvers via autoformalization interfaces.

The experiments show that LLMs are effective at predicting the necessary formal reasoning strategies with accuracy above 90 percent, and the framework outperformed competing baselines by 27 percent and 6 percent compared to GPT-4o and DeepSeek-V3.1, respectively. Additionally, adaptive reasoning can positively impact pure LLM methods, yielding gains of 10, 5, and 6 percent on zero-shot, CoT, and symbolic CoT settings with GPT-4o.

The code and data are available at https://github.com/idiap/adaptive_symbolic_reasoning.

**Key Findings on Self-Consistency Limitations:**

Existing self-consistency implementations primarily focus on final answer verification, neglecting the logical coherence of intermediate reasoning steps—a critical limitation for complex mathematical tasks that require step-by-step derivation.

As model size and task complexity increase, the time and computation cost of self-consistency increase sharply, making it a critical issue in practical applications.

A wide range of pre-trained language models demonstrate low consistency rates on hypothetical and compositional transformations.

Based on the search results, here's what I found about DuPO:

## DuPO: Dual Learning-based Preference Optimization

DuPO is a dual learning-based preference optimization framework that generates annotation-free feedback via a generalized duality.

### Key Approach

DuPO decomposes a primal task's input into known and unknown components, then constructs its dual task to reconstruct the unknown part using the primal output and known information (e.g., reversing math solutions to recover hidden variables), broadening applicability to non-invertible tasks. The quality of this reconstruction serves as a self-supervised reward to optimize the primal task, synergizing with LLMs' ability to instantiate both tasks via a single model.

### Problem It Addresses

I found the paper you were looking for! The paper is titled "Self-Consistency Falls Short! The Adverse Effects of Positional Bias on Long-Context Problems" by Adam Byerly and Daniel Khashabi. 

The paper examines how self-consistency (SC) improves the performance of large language models (LLMs) across various tasks and domains that involve short content, but challenges the assumption that SC's benefits generalize to long-context settings, finding that SC not only fails to improve but actively degrades performance on long-context tasks.

The paper is available at: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.01101

Evaluating Algorithmic Reasoning with   Counterexample Creation","url":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2502.19414"},{"title":"Single LLM Debate, MoLaCE: Mixture of Latent Concept Experts Against Confirmation Bias","url":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2512.23518"}]

I found the paper! Here are the search results:

The paper "Failing to Falsify: Evaluating and Mitigating Confirmation Bias in Language Models" by Ayush Rajesh Jhaveri and colleagues finds that across eleven LLMs of multiple families and scales, LLMs exhibit confirmation bias, often proposing triples to confirm their hypothesis rather than trying to falsify it.

This leads to slower and less frequent discovery of the hidden rule. The researchers found that prompting LLMs with intervention instructions consistently decreases confirmation bias, improving rule discovery rates from 42% to 56% on average.

They further mitigate confirmation bias by distilling intervention-induced behavior into LLMs, showing promising generalization to a new task, the Blicket test.

The paper was posted on arXiv on April 2, 2026 (arXiv:2604.02485).

**NSVIF** - a neuro-symbolic framework for verifying whether an LLM's output follows the instructions used to prompt the LLM. This paper was submitted on January 25, 2026.

NSVIF formulates instruction-following verification as a constraint-satisfaction problem by modeling user instructions as constraints. It is a universal, general-purpose verifier that makes no assumption on the instruction or the LLM.

Links: [{"title":"Eidoku: A Neuro-Symbolic Verification Gate for LLM Reasoning via Structural Constraint Satisfaction","url":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2512.20664"},{"title":"Uncovering Hidden Correctness in LLM Causal Reasoning via Symbolic Verification","url":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2601.21210"},{"title":"Reasoning Core: A Scalable RL Environment for LLM Symbolic Reasoning","url":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2509.18083"},{"title":"Published as a conference paper at ICLR 2026","url":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2508.05004"},{"title":"Incentivizing LLMs to Self-Verify Their Answers","url":"https://arxiv.org/html/2506.01369v1"}]

Based on the search results, I found several relevant papers from 2026 related to LLM verification and symbolic reasoning:

1. **Eidoku: A Neuro-Symbolic Verification Gate for LLM Reasoning** - This work reformulates the verification of LLM reasoning as a Constraint Satisfaction Problem (CSP) operating independently of the generation likelihood. EIDOKU operates on an evaluation axis that is optimization-independent to the generation objective, while System 1 optimizes for plausibility, System 2 evaluates structural feasibility.

2. **Uncovering Hidden Correctness in LLM Causal Reasoning via Symbolic Verification** - A symbolic verifier checks whether a model-generated causal expression is semantically equivalent to the ground truth under a given DAG, exploring all valid derivations using do-calculus and probability rules to identify formal equivalence.

3. **Reasoning Core: A Scalable RL Environment for LLM Symbolic Reasoning** - This provides a source of high-quality, verifiable data that targets core, generalizable symbolic reasoning skills by interfacing with external solvers to ensure rigorous standards of correctness.

These papers all address different aspects of independent verification and symbolic reasoning for LLMs, with publication dates around 2026.

# Paper Details

**Authors:** Jason Chan, Robert Gaizauskas, and Zhixue Zhao

**Title:** "Position: Logical Soundness is not a Reliable Criterion for Neurosymbolic Fact-Checking with LLMs"

**Publication Date:** April 5, 2026 (v1); April 25, 2026 (v2)

**Topic:** Yes, this paper directly addresses neurosymbolic fact-checking and logical soundness. The authors argue that "formal logic is often proposed as a rigorous means by which to mitigate bias, errors and hallucinations" in LLM-based fact-checking systems, yet they contend that approaches relying on logical soundness "structurally fail to detect misleading claims." The paper was submitted to the ICLR 2026 Workshop on Logical Reasoning of Large Language Models.

# Paper Summary

**Authors:** Chenchen Kuai, Jiwan Jiang, Zihao Zhu, Hao Wang, Keshu Wu, Zihao Li, Yunlong Zhang, Chenxi Liu, Zhengzhong Tu, Zhiwen Fan, and Yang Zhou

**Title:** "How Independent are Large Language Models? A Statistical Framework for Auditing Behavioral Entanglement and Reweighting Verifier Ensembles"

**Submission Date:** April 8, 2026

**Topic:** Yes, this work directly addresses LLM independence and behavioral entanglement. The abstract states the paper develops "a statistical framework for auditing behavioral entanglement among black-box LLMs" and investigates whether seemingly different models exhibit "hidden behavioral dependencies, latent entanglement, that undermine multi-model systems."

The research examines 18 LLMs across six model families, proposing metrics to measure dependencies and demonstrating practical improvements in ensemble verification through de-entangled reweighting methods.

I found the paper you're looking for! The paper "Self-Consistency Falls Short! The Adverse Effects of Positional Bias on Long-Context Problems" is by Adam Byerly and Daniel Khashabi and was published on arXiv (2411.01101).

Self-consistency (SC) improves the performance of large language models (LLMs) across various tasks and domains that involve short content, but SC not only fails to improve but actively degrades performance on long-context tasks. 

The paper challenges the assumption that SC's benefits generalize to long-context settings, where LLMs often struggle with position bias, the systematic over-reliance on specific context regions-which hinders their ability to utilize information effectively from all parts of their context. This degradation is driven by persistent position bias, which worsens with longer context lengths and smaller model sizes but remains invariant to prompt format or task type. 1	\section{Discussion and Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	\label{sec:conclusion}
4	
5	\looseness=-1
6	Our central finding is that the value of formal verification depends on the \emph{independence} of its inputs, not the soundness of its logic. \emph{SICA-specific}: under source-attribution scoring, SICA reduces to majority vote (Observation~1), and 27 training-free variants fail to escape. \emph{General}: the DPI bound (Remark~1) applies to any deterministic self-extraction, and SCB (BR $> 1$) holds across nine models (7B to frontier), suggesting the bottleneck is intrinsic to self-referential constraint generation. Three diagnostics ($K_\text{eff}$, BR, $C_0$) correctly characterize failure modes across all 60 tested conditions, with boundaries generalizing to held-out families (\S\ref{sec:diagnostic-framework}).
7	
8	\looseness=-1
9	This has direct consequences for test-time compute scaling \citep{snell2025scaling}: with $K_\text{eff} \leq 3.62$ for $K{=}12$, same-model traces scale redundancy, not information, advancing beyond \citet{huang2024selfcorrect}'s empirical findings to formal self-verification. Escaping the bottleneck requires structural independence (\S\ref{sec:cross-arch}); whether trained extractors or scaled cross-architecture verification can shift this boundary remains open.
10

1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related-work}
3	
4	\looseness=-1
5	\paragraph{Self-consistency and ensemble theory.}
6	Self-consistency (SC) selects the most frequent answer across chain-of-thought traces \citep{wang2023sc}. Extensions address free-form generation \citep{chen2024usc}, confidence weighting \citep{taubenfeld2025cisc}, diverse prompting \citep{li2023diverse}, and reasoning-aware filtering \citep{wan2024rasc, toh2024notallvotes, kim2026reasc}. SC gains plateau early \citep{loo2025reevaluating, byerly2026selfconsistency}. The Condorcet Jury Theorem \citep{condorcet1785} guarantees majority-vote convergence under independence; \citet{ladha1992correlated} show correlated voters reduce effective ensemble size. Our $K_\text{eff}$ adapts Fleiss' $\kappa$ from ensemble diversity theory \citep{kuncheva2003measures, brown2005diversity} to quantify this compression (\S\ref{sec:pipeline}). \citet{kim2025correlated} demonstrate correlated errors in LLM outputs; \citet{kuai2026independent} propose cross-model independence auditing, complementary to our within-model process-$\kappa$.
7	
8	\looseness=-1
9	\paragraph{Neuro-symbolic reasoning.}
10	We distinguish three paradigms: \emph{specification-generation} \citep{pan2023logiclm, olausson2023linc, ye2024satlm} translates problems into formal specifications before answering; \emph{program-generation} \citep{gao2023pal, chen2023program, lyu2023faithful} produces executable code; both formalize \emph{before} answer generation. \emph{Trace-extraction} (this work) operates post-hoc, inheriting confirmation bias (\S\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}). Recent extensions expand these paradigms with symbolic planning, logic agents, and verified reasoning \citep{ryu2025clover, xu2026dylos, li2025aristotle, wang2025fover, singh2026verge, luo2025logicagent, miya2025eidoku}; \citet{amonkar2025realitycheck} show formalization quality degrades with problem complexity; \citet{chan2026position} argue logical soundness alone is unrelia

1	\section{Escaping the Bottleneck}
2	\label{sec:solutions}
3	\label{sec:cross-arch}
4	
5	\paragraph{Motivation.}
6	\looseness=-1 No training-free self-extraction method achieves independence from the model's distributional prior (\S\ref{sec:remediation}). Remark~1 implies that any escape must break the self-extraction dependency: the information ceiling $I(C;Y) \leq I(T;Y)$ binds whenever constraints are deterministic functions of the generating model's traces. We test whether a structurally independent verifier, sharing neither architecture nor training data with the generator, provides corrective signal that escapes this bound.
7	
8	\paragraph{Method.}
9	\looseness=-1 We use DeBERTa-large \citep{he2021deberta} (406M, encoder-only), fine-tuned on MultiNLI \citep{williams2018mnli}, to classify (premise, conclusion) pairs as entailment, contradiction, or neutral. In combo mode, each NLI-supporting vote counts $w$ times alongside SC's $K$ votes. We evaluate on Mistral-7B FOLIO ($n = 204$) and ProofWriter depth-5 ($n = 600$).
10	
11	\paragraph{Zero-shot results.}
12	\looseness=-1 DeBERTa NLI alone achieves 49.51\% on FOLIO and 55.00\% on ProofWriter. Combo $w{=}3$ yields $+6.67$\,pp on ProofWriter for Mistral-7B ($p < 0.001$, significant after BH correction) and $+2.94$\,pp on FOLIO (n.s.); all generators with SC $\geq 72\%$ show no significant improvement ($\Delta \leq +0.83$\,pp). Same-model SICA \emph{degrades} ProofWriter ($-2.83$\,pp; Table~\ref{tab:main_results}), illustrating that corrective signal must originate outside the model's distribution.

(2024) + Condorcet framework 下是 predictable 的，而非 surprising。\n- 为什么影响接收：Excitement 评分直接取决于 novelty claim 是否成立。如果 SCB 是 Huang et al. 的 \"formal instantiation\" 而非 \"advance beyond\"，论文的贡献定位需要调整。\n- 建议修改方向：将 \"advances beyond\" 改为更准确的表述如 \"extends ... to the formal verification setting\" 或 \"provides structural formalization of\"。明确区分 scope extension vs. fundamental advance。\n\n**N3** [critical] Cross-architecture escape 的证据过于薄弱以支撑其作为独立贡献的定位\n\n- 具体位置：Section 6, Table 3, Section 7\n- 问题：\n  -
